The 10 extra production files (FARM_Y, FARM_Z, FARM_AA–FARM_AH) aren't in the registry, so they're left out.\n- **File formats:** files are a mix of UTF-16 and UTF-8. Each has three report lines before the header. Semicolon files use European numbers (`18.000,0000`).\n- **Which value column:** several files have the value column twice. I used the one named in the calibration config. Text entries like `N/A`, `timeout` and `ERR` are treated as missing.\n- **Timestamps:** two formats are accepted (`YYYY-MM-DD` and `DD.MM.YYYY`). Rows like `2024-13-40 88:99:00` can't be parsed and are dropped.\n- **Time zones:** every farm produces roughly 06:00–18:00 by the file clock, so timestamps are local time. I converted them to UTC using each farm's time zone before applying the reporting-day window.\n- **Duplicates:** repeated timestamps aren't exact copies. Each pair has a placeholder `888888.8` row followed by the real reading. I ran `drop_duplicates()` and then kept the last row per timestamp, so the real reading wins.\n- **Units:** values were converted to true readings (recorded minus offset), then to kWh. Power readings in kW are multiplied by the farm's sampling interval (5, 15 or 30 minutes).\n\n**Judgement calls that affect the numbers:**\n- **Negative values:** I dropped them after calibration, as the rule says.
